function	script	F_tt	{
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"main")) end;
	set .@w$,(charat(strnpcinfo(4),6))+((getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4)) > 9)?(substr(strnpcinfo(4),8,9)):(charat(strnpcinfo(4),getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4))-1)));
	return .@w$;
}

-	script	entrance#tt_main	-1,{
	end;

OnEnable:
	enablenpc();
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;

OnStartArena:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		set .@in$, "n";
		set .@in, 1;
	} else {
		set .@in$,(compare(strnpcinfo(1),"Expert"))?"e":"n";
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
	}
	warpwaitingpc "turbo_"+.@in$+"_"+.@in,298,161;
	donpcevent "Broadcast#"+.@in$+.@in+"::OnEnable";
	disablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
	
OnInit:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(0),"main")) end;
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		waitingroom "Solo Mode",60,"Solo Mode#n1::OnStartArena",1,1000,10;
	} else {
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
		waitingroom strnpcinfo(1),60,strnpcinfo(0)+"::OnStartArena",.@in,1000,10;
	}
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
}

turbo_e_4,298,167,0	script	Point#tt_main	-1,15,15,{
	end;

OnTouch_:
	if (tt_point < 28999) {
		set tt_point,tt_point+2;
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	}
	else
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	end;
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Point Give-Away Guy#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

	donpcevent "hunting#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";

	end;

OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Broadcast#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

		donpcevent "Solo Mode#n1::OnEnable";
	} else {
		donpcevent RName(.@w$)+"::OnEnable";
	}
	enablenpc "No_Unfair_Start#"+.@w$+"-1";
	enablenpc "No_Unfair_Start#"+.@w$+"-2";

	donpcevent "hunting#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";

	disablenpc "Flasher_Exit_1#"+.@w$;
	disablenpc "Flasher_Exit_2#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Master#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"Expert mode":"Normal mode")+" - "+.@s+" person";
		return .@rn$;
	}
}

	end;

	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"snake")) {
		setarray .@n, 279,284,279,268,279,260,287,288,287,280,287,264,295,284,295,268,295,260,303,288,303,280,303,264,311,284,311,268,311,260,347,288,347,280,347,264,355,284,355,268,355,260,363,288,363,280,363,264,371,284,371,268,371,260,379,288,379,280,379,264;
		for (set .@i, 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@n); set .@i, .@i + 2)
			monster strnpcinfo(4),.@n[.@i],.@n[.@i+1],"Archer Skeleton",1420,1;
	} else {
		setarray .@n_1$, "Munak","1610";
		setarray .@n_1, 47,87,47,87,24,74,24,74,67,42,67,42,60,70,60,70,32,51,32,51,30,25,30,25,62,20,62,20,216,378,218,360,223,361,243,342,247,364;
		setarray .@n_2$, "Bongun","1611";
		setarray .@n_2, 47,87,24,74,67,42,60,70,30,25,62,20,32,51;
		setarray .@n_3$, "Yao Jun","1612";
		setarray .@n_3, 68,56,26,46;
		while (.@c < 3) {
			for (set .@i, 0; .@i < getarraysize(getd(".@n_"+.@c)); set .@i, .@i + 2)
				monster strnpcinfo(4),getd(".@n_"+.@c+"["+.@i+"]"),getd(".@n_"+.@c+"["+(.@i+1)+"]"),getd(".@n_"+.@c+"$[0]"),atoi(getd(".@n_"+.@c+"$[1]")),1;
			set .@c, .@c + 1;
		}
	}

	end;
}

-	script	cos_end#tt_main	01,{
	function	GetNumber;
OnTouch_:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (.@w$ == "n1") set $@end_time,gettimetick(0);
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),strcharinfo(0) +"刚抵达终点！恭喜！",bc_map,"0xFFFF00";
	if (.@w$ != "n1") {
		setd "$ttnames$["+GetNumber(.@w$)+"]",strcharinfo(0);
		donpcevent "Turbo Track Guide::OnWin_"+.@w$;
	}
	warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
	if (.@w$ == "e4" || .@w$ == "n4" || .@w$ == "n1") {
		if (.@w$ == "e4") {
			donpcevent "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		} else {
			donpcevent "Guide#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		}
		donpcevent "Master#"+.@w$+"::OnDisable";
		disablenpc "Broadcast#"+.@w$;
	} else {
		if (.@w$ == "e8" || .@w$ == "n8") setarray .@pts, 28951,50;
		if (.@w$ == "e16" || .@w$ == "n16") setarray .@pts, 28941,60;
		if (tt_point < .@pts[0]) set tt_point,tt_point+.@pts[1];
		warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
		enablenpc "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end2";
	}
	disablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
	
	function	GetNumber	{
		setarray .@w_n$[1], "n4","n8","n16","e4","e8","e16";
		for (set .@i, 1; .@i <= getarraysize(.@w_n$); set .@i, .@i + 1) {
			if (getarg(0) == .@w_n$[.@i]) 
				return .@i;
		}		
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
}

	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"This is the ending broadcast for Turbo Track "+RName(.@w$)+".",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

	end;

	end;

	end;

	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");

		mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"In the meantime, winners must proceed to receive their rewards as soon as possible.",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	else
		mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"Players within the arena must be in ready to enter the warp.",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"Expert mode":"Normal mode")+" - "+.@s+" person";
		return .@rn$;
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "Winner Helper#TBT_"+.@w$;
	end;
}

turbo_n_1,384,167,1	script	Guide#TBT_n1	47,{
	set .@hour_start,$@start_time / 10000;
	set .@min_start,(($@start_time % 10000) / 100);
	set .@sec_start,$@start_time % 100;
	set .@hour_end,$@end_time / 10000;
	set .@min_end,(($@end_time % 10000) / 100);
	set .@sec_end,$@end_time % 100;
	if (hour_start == 23 && hour_end == 0) set .@hour_end,24;
	set .@st_to_sec,((.@hour_start * 3600) + (.@min_start * 60) + (.@sec_start));
	set .@et_to_sec,((.@hour_end * 3600) + (.@min_end * 60) + (.@sec_end));
	set .@record_time,(.@et_to_sec - .@st_to_sec);
	set .@record_hour,.@record_time / 3600;
	set .@record_min,((.@record_time % 3600) / 60);
	set .@record_sec,.@record_time % 60;
	if (.@record_min < 0) {
		mes "[向导]";
		mes "干得好！也许你没有";
		mes "创造新纪录，但你";
		mes "坚持到了终点。现在让我";

		percentheal 100,100;
		next;
		mes "[向导]";
		if (tt_point < 28991) {
			mes "你将获得";
			mes "10 点加速赛道点数~！";
			set tt_point,tt_point+10;
			set my_point,tt_point;

		}
		else {
			mes "遗憾的是，我不能给你";
			mes "加速赛道点数，";
			mes "因为你会超过";
			mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
			mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
		}
		next;
		mes "[向导]";
		mes "感谢你";
		mes "参加";
		mes "加速赛道。";
		mes "你很快会被引导";
		mes "前往等候室。";
		close2;
		warp "turbo_room",72,89;
		end;
	}
	else {
		set .@topbun,(($ttranks[0] % 3600) / 60 );
		set .@topcho,$ttranks[0] % 60;
		set .@gap,$ttranks[0] - .@record_time;
		if (.@gap < 0) {
			mes "[向导]";
			mes "干得好！也许你没有";
			mes "创造新纪录，但你";
			mes "坚持到了终点。现在让我";
			mes "帮你消除疲劳~";
			percentheal 100,100;
			next;
			mes "[向导]";
			if (tt_point < 28991) {
				mes "你将获得";
				mes "10 点加速赛道点数~！";
				set tt_point,tt_point+10;
				set my_point,tt_point;
				mes "你现在共有";
				mes tt_point + " 点加速赛道点数。";
			}
			else {
				mes "遗憾的是，我不能给你";
				mes "加速赛道点数，";
				mes "因为你会超过";
				mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
				mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
			}
			next;
			mes "[向导]";
			mes "感谢你";
			mes "参加";
			mes "加速赛道。";
			mes "你很快会被引导";
			mes "前往等候室。";
			close2;
			warp "turbo_room",72,89;
			end;
		}
		else {
			mes "[向导]";
			mes "恭喜！";
			mes "你在单人模式中";
			mes "取得了第一名！";
			mes "你的名字将被记录";
			mes "在我们的记录中，"+ strcharinfo(0) +"~";
			setarray $ttranks[0],.@record_time;
			setarray $ttnames$[0],strcharinfo(0);
			next;
			if (tt_point < 28961) {
				mes "[向导]";
				mes "由于你创造了";
				mes "新的纪录，";
				mes "你将获得";
				mes "40 点加速赛道点数！";

				mes "遗憾的是，我不能给你";

	enablenpc "bing2#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

			mes "Turbo Track 比赛中无法使用以下技能：";
			mes "强夺、瞬间移动、传送之阵、隐匿、伪装、潜行、治愈、冰墙、狂暴、圣母之颂歌、庇护祝福和长矛冲锋。";
			mes "";
			next;
			//this appears supposed to fall through.
		case 5:
			mes "[Turbo Track Guide]";
		mes "请享受你在艾尔迪巴兰 Turbo Track 的时光。谢谢～";
		mes "";
		mes "";
			close;
		}
	}

OnInit:
	// This will only ever be run ONCE!
	// Unless you eradicate the variable.
	//
	// This OnInit initiallizes and sets the starting
	// data for the Turbo Track's "Record" Logs.
	// WARNING/ATTENTION: DO NOT MODIFY ANY OF THE DATA.
	// DOING SO WILL CAUSE INTERNAL PROBLEMS IN THE SCRIPT.
	if ($ttnames$[0] == "") {
		setarray $ttnames$[0],"Breezy Havana";	// Solo Mode/Time Attack
		setarray $ttnames$[1],"RS125";	// Normal 4 Player
		setarray $ttnames$[2],"Hollgrehenn";	// Normal 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[3],"Antonio";	// Noomal 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[4],"Aragham";	// Expert 4 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[5],"Kafra Jasmine";	// Expert 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[6],"Chris";	// Expert 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[7],"Breezy Havana";	// Honor Place 1
		setarray $ttnames$[8],"RS125";	// Honor Place 2

		setarray $ttnames$[9],"Breezy Havana";	// Top 1
		setarray $ttnames$[10],"Nari";	// Top 2
		setarray $ttnames$[11],"Senorita Sylvia";	// Top 3
		setarray $ttnames$[12],"Joo Jahk";	// Top 4
		setarray $ttnames$[13],"RS125";	// Top 5

		setarray $ttranks[0],999999,0,0,0,0,0;
	}
	end;

			mes "[物品兑换助手]";
			mes "请选择要用极速赛点数兑换的";
			mes "物品。";
			mes "你的极速赛点数。";
			next;
			switch(select("卡普拉传送免费券:五级魔法卷轴:经验值:三克拉钻石:礼盒:速度药水:取消。")) {
			case 1:
				mes "[物品兑换助手]";

					mes "[物品兑换助手]";
					mes "你已经";

					mes "你的请求。";
					close;
				}
				else if (.@input < 0 || .@input > 50) {

				next;
				mes "[物品兑换助手]";
				mes "此物品兑换服务由卡普拉";
				mes "公司、铁匠公会、";
				mes "以及可可多赌场和";
				mes "艾尔迪巴兰公会城堡";
				mes "管理部门 Luina 提供。";
				mes "";
				close;
			}
		case 2:
			mes "[物品兑换助手]";
			mes "极速赛点数可以";
			mes "兑换为竞技场点数。";
			mes "最多可以保存";
			mes "^4D4DFF29,000点极速赛点数。";
			next;
			mes "[物品兑换助手]";
			mes "达到上限前，";
			mes "极速赛点数之前，";
			mes "建议先花掉一些，";
			mes "这样就能继续获得";
			mes "极速赛获胜后的点数奖励。";
			close;
		}
	}
}

turbo_room,106,117,3	script	Point Manager#tt	833,{
	mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
	mes "你好。";
	mes "你享受";
	mes "极速赛的时光吗？";
	mes "我能如何";
	mes "帮助你？";
	next;
	switch(select("查看点数:兑换点数:^660000兑换说明^000000")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "你当前拥有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 极速赛 Points";
		mes "以及 " + arena_point + " 点竞技场点数。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "你当前拥有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 极速赛 Points";
		mes "以及 " + arena_point + " 点竞技场点数。";
		next;
		mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
		mes "请选择以下竞技场点数兑换方式。兑换超过";
		mes "一次兑换 10 点竞技场点数时，";
		mes "只能兑换竞技场点数的";
		mes "^4D4DFF10 的倍数^000000。";
		next;
		switch(select("2 AP -> 1 TTP:4 AP -> 2 TTP:6 AP -> 3 TTP:8 AP -> 4 TTP:10 AP or more:Cancel")) {
		case 1:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28999,2,1;
		case 2:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28998,4,2;
		case 3:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28997,6,2;
		case 4:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28996,8,4;
		case 5:
			mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
			mes "请输入将 10 点竞技场点数兑换为极速赛点数的次数，最多";
			mes "为 20。取消请输入“^3355FF0^000000”。";
			next;
			input .@input;
			if (.@input <= 0) {
				mes "[极速赛点数管理员]";
				mes "你已经";
				mes "取消了";
				mes "你的请求。";
				close;
			}
			else if (.@input > 20) {
				mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
				mes "你的请求超过";
				mes "最大限制。请输入";
				mes "不超过 20 的数值。";
				close;
			}
			else {
				set .@want_point1,10 * .@input;
				set .@want_point,5 * .@input;
				set .@my_turbo_all,tt_point+.@want_point;
				set .@my_arena_all,arena_point-.@want_point1;
				if (.@my_turbo_all > 28999) {
					mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
					mes "继续兑换会超过加速赛道点数上限。请先消费更多";
					mes "加速赛道点数后再使用服务。谢谢。";
					close;
				}
				if (.@my_arena_all < 0) {
					mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
					mes "抱歉，你没有";
					mes "足够的竞技场点数";
					mes "进行此次加速赛道点数";
					mes "兑换。";
					close;
				}
				else {
					mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
					mes "你已兑换";
					mes "10 点竞技场点数为";
					mes "加速赛道点数，次数：" + .@input + " 次。";
					mes "总计 " + .@want_point1 + " 竞技场点数";
					mes "已兑换为";
					mes "" + .@want_point +" 加速赛道点数。";
					set arena_point,.@my_arena_all; //arena_point
					set tt_point,.@my_turbo_all;
					next;
					mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
					mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
					mes "你现在拥有";
					mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 加速赛道点数";
					mes "以及 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 竞技场点数。";
					mes "感谢惠顾。";
					close;
				}
			}
		case 6:
			mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
			mes "你已";
			mes "取消";
			mes "请求。";
			close;
		}
	case 3:
		// There is no dialog for this option.
		// It's possible it's missing, or maybe
		// the dialog was never translated. XD
		//??
		close;
	}

S_ExchangePoints:
	if (tt_point > getarg(0)) {
		mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
		mes "继续兑换会超过加速赛道点数上限。请先消费更多";
		mes "加速赛道点数后再使用服务。谢谢。";
		close;
	}
	if (arena_point >= getarg(1)) {
		mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
		mes "按要求，";
		mes "2 竞技场点数";
		mes "已兑换";
		mes "为 1 点加速赛道点数。";
		set arena_point,arena_point-getarg(1);
		set tt_point,tt_point+getarg(2);
		next;
		mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + ",";
		mes "你现在拥有";
		mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 加速赛道点数";
		mes "以及 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 竞技场点数。";
		mes "感谢惠顾。";
		close;
	}
	else {
		mes "[加速赛道点数管理员]";
		mes "抱歉，你没有足够的竞技场点数。使用此服务";
		mes "至少需要 2 点竞技场点数。";
		mes "";
		close;
	}
}

alde_gld,183,204,0	script	en_turbo	45,1,1,{
OnTouch_:
	if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "^3355FF等一下！";
		mes "请稍候。";
		mes "你现在携带的物品";
		mes "太多了。";
		mes "请使用卡普拉服务";
		mes "存放一些物品后再回来。^000000";
		close;
	}
	if ((BaseJob == Job_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Crusader) && (checkriding() || checkdragon())) {
		if (MaxWeight-Weight < 20000) {
			if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
				set .@mount$,(BaseJob == Job_Knight)?"Peco Peco":"Grand Peco";
				mes "[助手]";
				mes .@mount$+"禁止进入";
				mes "加速赛道竞技场。";
				mes "请从你的";
				mes .@mount$+"上下来，";
				mes "你会获得免费大嘴鸟骑乘券";
				mes "用于取回你的"+.@mount$+"。";
			} else {
				set .@mount$,(BaseJob == Job_Knight)?"Dragon":"Gryphon";
				mes "[助手]";
				mes .@mount$+"禁止进入";
				mes "加速赛道竞技场。";
				mes "请从你的";
				mes .@mount$+"上下来。你可以在外面";
				mes "免费取回它。";
			}
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
				setriding 0;
				getitem 7310,1; //Free_Peco_Ticket
			} else {
				if (BaseJob == Job_Knight)
					setdragon 0;
				else
					setriding 0;
			}
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else if ((Class == Job_Ranger || Class == Job_Ranger_T || Class == Job_Baby_Ranger) && checkoption(Option_Wugrider)) {
		if (MaxWeight-Weight < 20000) {
			mes "[助手]";
			mes "狼骑兵禁止进入";
			mes "加速赛道竞技场。";
			mes "请从狼身上下来。";
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			setoption Option_Wugrider,0;
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else if ((Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) && checkmadogear()) {
		if (MaxWeight-Weight < 20000) {
			mes "[助手]";
			mes "魔导机甲禁止进入";
			mes "加速赛道竞技场。";
			mes "请从你的";
			mes "魔导机甲上下来。你可以在外面";
			mes "免费取回它。";
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			setmadogear 0;
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else
		warp "turbo_room",100,65;
	end;
}

-	script	::MountManager_turbo	-1,{
	set .@n$, "["+strnpcinfo(1)+"]";
	mes .@n$;
	if (ismounting()) {
		mes "请先从当前骑乘的坐骑上下来。";
		mes "然后再与我交谈。";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseJob == Job_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Crusader) {
		if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@zeny,2500;
				set .@zeny$,"2,500";
				set .@mount$,"大嘴鸟";
			} else {
				set .@zeny,3500;
				set .@zeny$,"3,500";
				set .@mount$,"巨型大嘴鸟";
			}
			set .@skill,63;
			set .@skill$,"大嘴鸟骑乘";
			set .@riding, checkriding();
			set .@i,1;
		} else {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@mount$,"龙";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"龙驯养术";
				set .@riding, checkdragon();
				set .@i,2;
			} else {
				set .@mount$,"狮鹫";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"大嘴鸟骑乘";
				set .@riding, checkriding();
				set .@i,1;
			}
		}
	} else if (Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) {
		set .@mount$,"魔导机甲";
		set .@skill,2255;
		set .@skill$,"魔导机甲执照";
		set .@riding, checkmadogear();
		set .@i,3;
	} else {
		mes "感谢你";
		mes "光临艾尔帕兰的";
		mes "加速赛道~";
		close;
	}
	mes "欢迎，";
	mes "你想取回你的"+.@mount$+"吗？";
	if (.@zeny) {
		mes "请出示大嘴鸟骑乘";
		mes "免费券。你也可以";
		mes "支付租赁费"+.@zeny$+" Zeny。";
		mes "即可。";
	}
	next;
	switch(select("是：取消")) {
	case 1:
		if (getskilllv(.@skill) == 0) {
			mes .@n$;
			mes "抱歉，";
			mes "你没有资格使用此服务。";
			mes "请先学习";
			mes .@skill$+"技能。";
			close;
		} else {
			if (.@riding) {
				mes .@n$;
				mes "你已经";
				mes "骑乘着";
				mes .@mount$+"。";
				mes "谢谢~";
				close;
			} else {
				if (.@zeny) {
					if (countitem(7310) < 1 && Zeny < .@zeny) {
						mes .@n$;
						mes "抱歉，你没有大嘴鸟骑乘免费券";
						mes "或足够的"+.@zeny$+" Zeny。";
						mes "无法使用大嘴鸟租赁服务。";
						mes "请准备好所需物品。";
						close;
					} else {
						if (countitem(7310) > 0)
							delitem 7310,1; //Free_Peco_Ticket
						else
							set Zeny, Zeny-.@zeny;
					}
				}
				switch(.@i) {
					case 1: setriding; break;
					case 2: setdragon; break;
					case 3: setmadogear; break;
				}
				mes .@n$;
				mes "感谢";
				mes "惠顾~";
				close;
			}
		}
	case 2:
		mes .@n$;
		mes "你要回去参加";
		mes "比赛";
		mes "加速赛道比赛吗？";
		mes "祝你好运！";
		close;
	}
}
